He proposed to display great annoyance at being saddled at the outset with such a labour, and, on being asked the reason for our resolve, to say that last night we had drawn up the remains of a baby, which meant, of course, that the whole of the water was foul. He would then procure ropes and buckets and other implements, and even give out that, if after one or two days we needed assistance, he should desire the landlord to find us some men.

So he and Carson left at half past eight, taking the Rolls, whilst Hanbury and I returned at once to the meadow to set to work upon the troughs. With nails and hammer these were easy to make, and, as soon as Bell appeared, we gave him a pick and shovel, and set him to cutting the groove in which our gutter should lie.

Within the hour the bell by the well told us of Mansel’s return, and five minutes later we saw him appear in the meadow and come towards us.

===Chinese===
随后我们回到马厩，那里存着些锯好的木料；早饭之前，一根横梁已架在圆顶正底下空着的柱子上，两侧也各捆牢一只滑轮。我们干这桩的当儿，卡森和贝尔正往上搬木板——这样的板子我们找出一大批，预备拿它们做成一道首尾相接的长槽，好把水引走。等我们议定了水槽的走向，已到七点，便把活计搁下，回宅里去。

早饭桌上，我们定下一件要紧事。这事很明白：我们决计不掩饰掏干这口井的意图。于是议定，曼塞尔一洗完澡便驱车去勒拉，把我们的打算告诉客栈老板。他打算一上来就满脸懊恼，抱怨起头就背上这样一桩苦工；若有人问起我们为何如此，便说昨夜我们从井里捞上来一个婴儿的残骸——不用说，整井的水都污了。随后他去置办绳索、水桶和别的家什，甚至放出风去：一两天后我们若要帮手，就要请老板给我们寻几个人来。

于是八点半，他同卡森开着劳斯莱斯去了；我和汉伯里立刻回到草地上，动手做槽。钉子锤子一使，槽板好做得很；贝尔一露面，我们又给他一把镐、一把锹，叫他去挖那道安放水槽的槽沟。

不出一个钟头，井边的铃响了，报知曼塞尔回来；又过五分钟，我们望见他出现在草地上，朝我们走来。

===Original===
“Did that bell ring?” he said shortly.

“Yes,” said Hanbury.

“Then, if you please,” said Mansel, “never disregard it again.”

And there he left it, for, as I have said, he was a man of few words: but there was that in his voice which there was no mistaking, and Hanbury and I felt as guilty and ashamed as though by some folly we had ruined our enterprise.

===Chinese===
「方才那铃响了没有？」他劈头便问。

「响了，」汉伯里说。

「那么，劳驾，」曼塞尔说，「下回再别把它不当回事。」

话说到这里便止住了：他这个人，我已说过，向来不多费唇舌；可他话音里的那股劲，谁也不会听岔。我和汉伯里又愧又臊，仿佛是我们一时糊涂，把整桩事业给毁了。

===Original===
Then he told us that his tale had been well received, and that the landlord seemed genuinely distressed at our predicament, “for such,” said Mansel, “he plainly regards it: and when I said that we must empty the well, he threw up his hands. However, when I said that, unless the well could be cleaned, I should give up the place, he sent for buckets and ropes, and promised to come up this evening to see what help he could give. I then sent a wire to Maple’s, telling them not to dispatch my furniture: I fear it may puzzle them, but it supported my case and cleared the air.”

Here Carson appeared with a bucket as big as a bath: indeed, two men could not have carried it full, but it was made, I imagine, to receive refuse or to hold a store of water upon which a cook could draw. And I must frankly confess that it did my heart good to see it, for I felt that, if we had a giant’s task before us, it was something, at any rate, to have a giant’s tools to use.

By the time we had finished the gutter, which led down out of the meadow into a kind of combe, it was nearly eleven o’clock, and we sat down and drank some beer which Carson had brought.

===Chinese===
随后他告诉我们，他那套说法人家很买账，老板对我们这步困境像是真心过意不去。「因为他分明就是这样看这件事的，」曼塞尔说。「我一说我们非掏干这井不可，他两手一摊。不过等我说，这井要是弄不干净，我只好退掉这个地方，他立刻叫人去取水桶绳索，还答应今晚就上来，看看能帮什么忙。跟着我又给梅普尔公司（Maple's）拍了封电报，叫他们先别发运我的家具；我怕他们会纳闷，可这一下把我的话坐实了，也把空气澄清了。」

正说到这里，卡森提着一只澡盆大小的桶来了；说真的，装满了两个人也抬不动，不过我想，它原是盛垃圾用的，或是存一桶水，供厨子随取随用。说句老实话，一见它我心里就痛快，因为我觉得，纵然摆在我们面前的是巨人的差事，手里总算有巨人的家伙可使，到底强些。

等水槽完工——它从草地往下，引向一道谷地模样的洼处——已快十一点，我们坐下来，喝了些卡森带来的啤酒。

===Original===
Then Bell was sent to the kitchen, and Rowley came up in his stead; Mansel went up to the point in the farther wood, from which, as I have said, a man could observe some of the neighbouring ground; and Carson and Rowley took one rope, and Hanbury and I the other.

The work was hard to the point of severity, but the sight of so much water coming up out of the well was fuel to our endeavours, and we worked for twenty minutes without a break. Long before this Mansel had come to our assistance, for to land the buckets, when full, needed another man, and, lame as he was, he performed this awkward task as he did most things, that is to say, as though he had practised it all his life.

When we stopped for luncheon, we had taken eight feet of water out of the well.

===Chinese===
于是贝尔被派回厨房，罗利（Rowley）顶了上来；曼塞尔上到远处林中那处地点——我说过，从那里可以瞭望邻近的一些地面；卡森同罗利拉一条绳，我和汉伯里拉另一条。

这活儿苦得近乎严酷；可眼见这么多水从井里提上来，正给我们添柴加油，我们一口气干了二十分钟，没有停歇。还没等歇，曼塞尔早来搭手了，因为满桶落地，须有第二个人接；他虽然瘸着，这桩别扭活计做来却同他做多数事情一样，也就是说，仿佛一辈子就在干这个。

到停下吃午饭时，我们已从井里提走了八英尺深的水。

===Original===
When luncheon was over, we sat on the terrace for a little, under the shade of the limes.

Hanbury was asleep, Mansel was reading *Lockhart’s Life of Scott*﻿—without which work, he said, he never travelled, because it was the best host a man’s mind could have﻿—and I was lazily regarding the opposing country, when I noticed a patch of haze a great way off. I had hardly remarked it before it disappeared, and it came to me in a flash that it must have been dust. At once I rose and took the binocular, and, since I knew precisely where to look for the road, I was in time to see a closed car flash into and out of view on its journey south.

When I told Mansel, he nodded and said he was glad.

===Chinese===
午饭一过，我们在露台上小坐片刻，头顶是菩提树的浓荫。

汉伯里睡着了；曼塞尔在读*洛克哈特的《司各特传》*——他说，出门他从不离它，因为它是人心所能有的最好的东道主——我则懒懒地望着对面的山野，忽见远处有一片雾似的什么东西。我刚留意到，它便没了；我心中一亮：那必是尘土。我立刻起身，抓过望远镜；好在路在何处我早心中有数，正好看见一辆篷车一闪而过，往南去了。

我告诉了曼塞尔；他点点头，说他高兴。

===Original===
“It’s high time,” said he. “If they hadn’t appeared today, I should have been uneasy. I like the other side to do the obvious thing.”

With that he put up his volume, and, asking me to tell him when the car reached the spot from which we had first seen Wagensburg, rose to his feet and began to pace the courtyard, with his hands behind his back and his head in the air.

Presently the closed car appeared beyond the river, and when I reported this, Mansel called the servants, and I roused Hanbury and told him what was afoot.

Then Mansel spoke to us all.

===Chinese===
「早该来了，」他说。「他们今天再不露面，我心里就要不安稳了。我喜欢对面的人做那种一眼看得穿的事。」

说罢他合上那卷书，叫我等那辆车到了我们初见瓦根斯堡的那处地方便告诉他，随即站起身，倒背着手，昂着头，在院子里踱起步来。

不多时，那辆篷车在河那边出现了；我一报信，曼塞尔便唤齐了仆人，我又推醒汉伯里，把眼下的动静告诉他。

于是曼塞尔对大伙说话。

===Original===
“We’re going to be visited,” he said, “by five very angry men. I think there’ll be five, and I’m sure they’ll be angry. This is a good place to receive them for several reasons. I think perhaps I’d better play host, but I shall want some support. Mr. Hanbury will take the gateway, and Mr. Chandos the road: Carson will take that window, and Rowley that: and Bell will occupy the loft. Please be ready, but nobody show himself until you hear me say ‘**Now.**’ And whatever happens, don’t fire. I believe in baring the teeth, but to use them, except to bite back, would be very foolish.”

Then the stable doors were opened, the Rolls was brought out, and out of her we were armed﻿—the servants with sporting rifles, and Mansel, Hanbury and I with a pistol apiece.

The servants had been through the War, and took this quietly enough, but I never was so much excited in all my life, and pictures of blood-lettings and feats of arms rose up before me like so many common rooms of which I had been made free.

===Chinese===
「就要有五个怒气冲天的人来造访了，」他说。「我想是五个；他们一定怒气冲天，这个我拿得稳。就在这里接待他们，有几个道理。东道主我想还是我自己来做的好，不过也得有人帮衬。汉伯里先生守大门，钱多斯（Chandos）先生守大路；卡森守那扇窗，罗利守那扇；贝尔占住棚楼。请各位就位，可谁也别露头，除非听见我说一声『**现在**』。无论出了什么事，不许开枪。亮出牙来，我是信的；可除了还口咬，真咬下去就蠢透了。」

于是马厩的门开了，劳斯莱斯牵了出来；我们就从她身上武装起来——仆人们一人一杆猎枪，曼塞尔、汉伯里和我一人一支手枪。

仆人们都是打过大战的，受了这个安之若素；我却生平没有这样激动过，眼前浮现出一幅幅流血斗狠、逞勇弄枪的画面，仿佛一间接一间的公共厅堂，如今都许我随意出入。

===Original===
Then the car was returned to the stables, and the servants went to their posts, while Mansel showed Hanbury and me how a pistol should be handled, and that the safety-catch was the stile between life and death. After that, Mansel took Tester and shut him up in the house, and, when he came back, we sat down on the wall of the well, by the side of the house, until we should hear the car.

While we were there, Mansel inquired if there was any one line which we thought he should take in dealing with Ellis and his friends, “because,” said he, “beyond recommending them to return to the deuce, I’ve no plan at all. I don’t propose to deny that we’re looking for treasure, and I propose to announce that we’re cleaning the well. If you can’t conceal, advertise: it’s the next best thing. But I’ve little else in my mind, except that this courtyard is as much as they’re going to see.”

Hanbury and I had no suggestion to offer, if for no other reason, because to think at all clearly was beyond our power. This mean state of mind, I am sure, was due to our expectation of what was to come, and, since this failing is one which I have never cured, I have the more reverence for Mansel who, I think, could await the Powers of Darkness themselves without turning a hair.

Presently we heard the drone of a car climbing into the woods, and Hanbury and I passed out of the great gateway and sat down behind the chapel where we could not be seen.

As I afterwards found, the car contained five men, all of whom alighted, three only of whom spoke. These three were Ellis, the man whom I had knocked down, and the other who had answered Mansel at the level-crossing. The last was addressed by his companions as “Rose”﻿—Mansel told us later that he was undoubtedly “Rose” Noble, a man of some position among thieves﻿—and my friend was called “Punter,” though whether that was a nickname I cannot say.

===Chinese===
随后汽车送回马厩，仆人们各就各位；曼塞尔则指点我和汉伯里手枪该怎么使，又讲明保险栓是横在生死之间的那道栅门。此后，曼塞尔把泰斯特牵进屋里关好；他回来，我们便在宅旁井边的井墙上坐下，只等听见汽车的声音。

坐着的时候，曼塞尔问我们，对付埃利斯和他那伙朋友，他该不该定下一条什么路线，「因为，」他说，「除了奉劝他们回到魔鬼那里去，我满脑子没有一点章程。我不打算否认我们在找财宝，也不打算不宣布我们在清理这口井。瞒不住，就索性张扬出去：这是次一等的良策。此外我肚里没什么了，只有一条：这个院子，就是他们能看到的全部。」

我和汉伯里提不出什么主意，不说别的，单是要清清楚楚想上一想，我们已办不到。这种窝囊心境，我确信是因为我们一直在等那桩要来的事；而这毛病我至今没治好，所以我越发敬重曼塞尔——依我看，纵是黑暗诸神亲临，他也能等着，眉毛都不动一根。

不多时，我们听见一辆汽车嗡嗡着爬进树林；我和汉伯里出了大门，在小礼拜堂后面坐下，那里望不见我们。

后来我才知道，车里坐着五个人，人人下车，只有三个开口。这三个便是埃利斯、被我打翻的那一个，还有在铁路道口答过曼塞尔话的那一个。末了这一个，同伙们唤他「罗斯」——曼塞尔后来告诉我们，他无疑就是罗斯·诺布尔（Rose Noble），在贼帮里是个有头有脸的人物——而我那位朋友，他们唤作「潘特（Punter）」，是不是诨名，我可说不上。

===Original===
The car came to rest on the terrace, and we heard them alight, but for a moment or two they spoke between themselves, as though they had not seen Mansel, and believed the courtyard empty.

Then:

“Can I help you?” said Mansel.

When Ellis replied, his voice was shaking, and his speech thick with wrath.

“Yes,” he said, “you can. You can pop along off my land. That’ll save me the trouble of putting you out.”

“Oh, are you my landlord?” said Mansel. “Because, if you are, you can help me to clean out your well. It seems to have been used as a cemetery, and I didn’t come here to get typhoid.”

===Chinese===
汽车在露台上停稳，我们听见他们下车；可有片刻工夫，他们只在彼此交谈，仿佛没有看见曼塞尔，只当院子里空无一人。

这时：

「有什么要我效劳的么？」曼塞尔说。

埃利斯一开口，声音发颤，话里全是压不住的怒气。

「有的，」他说，「有的。你可以麻利儿从我的地皮上滚蛋。那倒省得我费事赶你。」

「哦，原来你是我的业主？」曼塞尔说。「那正好，你既是我业主，就请你出力，把你这口井给我淘干净。看来它是被人拿当过坟地的，我总不是到这儿来染伤寒的。」

===Original===
Ellis began to rave, but Rose Noble put him aside.

“What’s this wash about landlords?”

“It’s very simple,” said Mansel, stifling a yawn. “If he owns this estate, he’s my landlord. If he doesn’t, he isn’t. So in any event the question of putting me out will not arise. But I tell you frankly I’m fed up about this well. Supposing﻿—”

“Cut it out,” said Rose Noble. “Ellis here’s got you down. This place was for sale, and he’s bought it.”

===Chinese===
埃利斯破口便骂，罗斯·诺布尔把他拨到一边。

「这满口的业主业主，嚷嚷的是什么玩意儿？」

「再简单不过，」曼塞尔忍住一个哈欠说。「他要是这产业的主人，他便是我业主；他不是，便不是。所以无论如何，撵我走这一节根本谈不到。不过我跟你说句实话，这口井我已烦透了。假如——」

「少来这一套，」罗斯·诺布尔说。「这位埃利斯已经把你治得死死的。这地方原是出卖的，他买下了。」

===Original===
“And I hold a fifty years’ lease,” said Mansel. “If he wasn’t told, he should have been. But perhaps they thought if he knew he wouldn’t buy. And now about this well. When I took the place I was given to understand﻿—”

“You’re a great believer in bluff,” said Rose Noble.

“You don’t believe me?” said Mansel. “Well, that’s as you please. But if I’m not here of right, why did they give me the keys?”

There was a moment’s silence.

===Chinese===
「何况我手里握着一张五十年的租契，」曼塞尔说。「就算没人告诉他，也本该告诉他。不过，兴许他们琢磨着，他要是知道了，便不肯买了。再说这口井。我接这地方的时候，人家给我的意思是——」

「你这份虚张声势的本事，倒信得很虔诚，」罗斯·诺布尔说。

「你不信我？」曼塞尔说。「好，那随你的便。可我要是站在这儿没有名分，他们怎么把钥匙交到我手里？」

一时间没有人言语。

===Original===
Then:

“Lease be damned,” roared Ellis. “I’ve bought the —﻿—﻿— place.”

“That gives you,” said Mansel, “no shadow of right to be here. Unless I’m behind with my rent, you can’t set a foot on this land﻿—for fifty years.”

===Chinese===
跟着：

「租约见鬼去吧，」埃利斯吼道。「这地方我已经买下了，————的。」

「这可没给你半点儿立足此地的权利，」曼塞尔说。「只要我不欠租，你休想在这片地上落脚——五十年都是如此。」

===Original===
At this there was a great uproar, and I slipped into the road and up as close as I dared, to see that all was well.

Rose Noble and Punter were holding back Ellis, while Mansel was sitting still upon the rim of the well, with one leg cocked over the other, and a pipe in his mouth.

Presently the storm abated, and Ellis suffered Punter to lead him away to the car, on the step of which he sat down and mopped his face, while Rose Noble continued to play the hand.

===Chinese===
这一嗓子掀起一场大乱；我溜到路口，凑到敢凑的最近处，看是不是一切照旧。

罗斯·诺布尔和潘特死死拽住埃利斯，曼塞尔却端坐在井沿上，一条腿跷在另一条上，嘴里叼着烟斗。

不多时风暴平息了；埃利斯由着潘特把他领到汽车边，在脚踏板上坐下，抹着脸；罗斯·诺布尔接着打他手里的牌。

===Original===
“Leases and whatnot,” he said, “don’t cut much ice with me. The Law’s well enough in its place, but I guess we can do without it this afternoon.”

“If you mean,” said Mansel, “that you want to stay here and talk, I won’t ask you to withdraw for a quarter of an hour.”

“That,” said Rose Noble quietly, “is exactly what I meant.”

With that, he took out a cigar and leaned his back against a tree.

===Chinese===
「租约啦、什么啦，」他说，「在我这儿都吃不开。法律搁在它该搁的地方自然不坏；不过我看，今天下午咱们可以不用它。」

「你的意思若是，」曼塞尔说，「想留在这里说说话，那么一刻钟之内，我不请你们退走。」

「我的意思，」罗斯·诺布尔不动声色地说，「正是如此。」

说罢他掏出一支雪茄，背靠着一棵树。

===Original===
“Do you seriously think,” he said, “that we’re going to sit right down and let you lift that treasure under our eyes?”

“Not for one moment,” said Mansel cheerfully.

“Then,” said Rose Noble, “why don’t you face the facts? We’re five to one and two boys: this isn’t exactly Holborn: and we’re not afraid to strike.”

“I know Ellis isn’t,” said Mansel.

===Chinese===
「你当真以为，」他说，「我们会安坐不动，由你当着我们的眼皮子底下，把那批财宝吊上去？」

「做梦也不会，」曼塞尔兴致很好地答道。

「那么，」罗斯·诺布尔说，「你怎么不面对事实？我们五个对一个，外带两个小子：这儿可不是霍尔本（Holborn）；我们动手可不发怵。」

「埃利斯不发怵，这个我知道，」曼塞尔说。

===Original===
At that Ellis started up with a volley of oaths, but Rose Noble cursed him into silence, and returned to the charge.

“You know where that treasure is?”

“I do and I don’t,” said Mansel. “To be perfectly frank, I was going to start looking today, but this infernal well has upset my plans. You must have water, you know.”

“Quit that line,” said the other sharply. “And tell me﻿—what do you know?”

===Chinese===
埃利斯一听跳起来，一串咒骂连珠炮似的喷出；罗斯·诺布尔一声断喝把他骂哑了，又接着进逼。

「你知道那批财宝在哪儿？」

「知道，又不知道，」曼塞尔说。「跟你交个底：我今天本打算着手去找，可这口该死的井搅了我的局。你们要知道，水总是离不了的。」

「收起这一套，」对方厉声说。「告诉我——你到底知道多少？」

===Original===
“Yes, I see the firearm,” said Mansel. “But it doesn’t faze me. Unless I misjudge you, you’re not going to make the mistake which was made not far from Chartres three weeks ago.”

Ellis leapt to his feet.

“How long are we going to stand this?” he cried. “Put it across the —﻿—﻿— once for all. Shove the cards on the table. I’m sick of being chewed.”

Rose Noble disregarded him.

===Chinese===
「不错，那家伙我瞧见了，」曼塞尔说。「可它吓不倒我。要是我没看错你，你是不会重蹈覆辙的——三个星期前，离沙特尔（Chartres）不远，就有人犯过一回。」

埃利斯一蹦而起。

「这样的气我们还要受多久？」他嚷道。「把那————的家伙狠狠治一回，治个一劳永逸。把牌摊到桌面上。人家嚼来嚼去，我受够了。」

罗斯·诺布尔只当没听见。

===Original===
“You drew on me,” he said quietly, “by the side of the railway line. You made a hole in my car.”

“Two,” said Mansel. “Two holes, counting the petrol-tank.”

The other lighted his cigar.

“Two holes,” he said slowly. “And Punter was knocked down. And in spite of all that, I’m going to give you your choice.” He threw away the match and folded his arms. “Give us your map or plan or note or whatever it is: give up possession quietly: give me your word to keep out of Austria for the next six months, and I’ll let the three of you go.”

===Chinese===
「你对我拔过枪，」他不动声色地说，「就在铁路线旁边。你在我车上打了一个洞。」

「两个，」曼塞尔说。「两个洞，油箱算在内。」

对方点着了雪茄。

「两个洞，」他慢悠悠地说。「潘特又挨了一下，叫人打翻在地。尽管如此，我还是要给你们一个选择。」他甩掉火柴，抱起双臂。「把你们的图也好、方案也好、笔录也好，不管是什么，交出来：安安静静把地方让出来：给我一句保证，往后六个月不踏进奥地利，我就放你们三个走。」

===Original===
“I see,” said Mansel. “What’s the alternative?”

“We take possession,” said Rose Noble, “here and now: you will stay *as our guests* until the treasure is found. How long that period will be will depend upon *your ability to withstand the inclination to drink*. When it has been found, and we are gone, your future will depend upon how long it is before somebody passes this way.”

I never heard words uttered in a tone so cold and merciless, and Ellis appeared almost genial beside this sinister man.

He was a big, hook-nosed fellow with sandy hair. His face was grey and flabby, and he was very fat. He had a curious way of hooding his eyes, but when he drew back his lids﻿—and this was seldom﻿—you seemed to be looking upon two coals of fire, that were consumed with hatred of everything they saw.

When he had spoken, there was a little silence.

===Chinese===
「明白，」曼塞尔说。「不答应又如何？」

「由我们接收，」罗斯·诺布尔说，「现在，当场：你们便*作我们的客人*，留到财宝找到为止。这段日子多长，要看*你们抵得住抵不住酒瘾*。等财宝到了手、我们也走了，你们的前程，便看过多久才有人打这条路上经过。」

这样冷毒的话音，我从未听人说过；站在这个阴险家伙身边，埃利斯倒显得几乎是和气的了。

他是个大块头，鹰钩鼻子，一头沙黄头发。脸色灰败松弛，人极胖。他有个怪癖，总把眼皮罩下来；可一旦撩起眼皮——这很少见——你就像看着两块火炭，它们燃着对所见一切的仇恨，烧尽为止。

他说完，片刻无人言语。

===Original===
Then:

“That’s the stuff,” said Punter, with half a laugh.

“You think so?” said Mansel swiftly. “Well, we shall see.” He rose. “And now I’m going to be less generous than you. I’m going to give you no choice﻿—except to withdraw. I’m not going to look for that treasure while you sit and watch me do it. I’m in no hurry: in fact, I’ve time to burn. I’ve taken a lease of this place for fifty years; the fishing round about here is such as I love, and at the present moment, though it doesn’t seem to interest you, I’ve got my hands full with this well. But don’t think, from what I say, that you’re free of these grounds. I’ve a right to order you off, and I’m going to do it *right* **now**. If after this, you return, you’ll return as trespassers, and you can take it from me that, so far as this estate is concerned, *trespassers will be shot*.”

===Chinese===
跟着：

「好，正该这样，」潘特半笑着咧嘴说。

「你当是这样？」曼塞尔应声便答。「好，那就走着瞧。」他站起身。「现在，轮到我了，我可不像你们那样慷慨。我不给你们选择——只除了退走。你们坐在那儿看我找，我就不找那批财宝。我不急；说真的，我的时间多得没处花。这地方我租下了五十年；四周的钓鱼去处正对我的胃口；而眼下，虽然看来诸位不感兴趣，单是这口井已经叫我忙得不可开交。可别听了我的话，就以为这片园子你们可以随意出入。我叫你们走，是有权利的，而我*就要*这么做——**现在**。这回之后你们再来，来时便是擅入者；这一点尽可信我：就这份产业而论，*擅入者格杀勿论*。」

===Original===
With that, he looked round the courtyard, and, seeing, I suppose, something in his movement which they did not understand, the five men followed his gaze.

Asprawl in the mouth of the loft, Bell was covering Rose Noble: each of the two open windows was framing a rifle-barrel, with a head and shoulders behind: and Hanbury stood in the gateway, and I was in the mouth of the road.

There was a long silence.

===Chinese===
说罢他环顾院子；他那动作里的什么名堂，大约是他们没有看懂的，五个人便随着他的目光望去。

贝尔大张着身子趴在棚楼口上，枪口正罩着罗斯·诺布尔；两扇敞开的窗户，各框着一截枪管，枪管后面是脑袋和肩膀；汉伯里立在大门里，我守在路口。

接着是一阵长久的静默。

===Original===
At length:

“That’s two tricks to you,” said Rose Noble, rubbing his nose. “But I don’t think you’ll get any more. An ace and a King look pretty, but they only take one trick each, and I’d rather hold the rest of the suit.”

“Are you quite sure you do?” said Mansel.

“Yes,” said Rose Noble, “and let me tell you this. Before the game’s over you’ll remember this afternoon﻿ ﻿… and the sunshine﻿ ﻿… and the air﻿ ﻿… and the pretty, bright blue sky﻿ ﻿… And when you remember them, you’ll curse the —﻿—﻿— that bore you, and﻿—”

===Chinese===
到末了：

「这两墩归你，」罗斯·诺布尔搓着鼻子说。「可我看你再吃不着了。一张爱司、一张老王，看着是漂亮，可各自只吃得一墩；这一门牌剩下的，我情愿捏在自己手里。」

「你真拿得稳么？」曼塞尔说。

「拿得稳，」罗斯·诺布尔说，「还有一句奉送。牌局终了之前，你会想起这个下午……想起这阳光……想起这空气……想起那片明媚的、亮蓝的天……一想起这些，你就会诅咒生养你的那个————东西，而且——」

===Original===
Mansel had knocked him down, and, pistol in hand, was flat against the trunk of the lime-tree against which Rose Noble had lately been leaning, before a man could cry out or a shot could be fired. I have never seen any movements so swiftly made; indeed, looking back at the episode, I cannot honestly say that I remember it in detail, for, though I was looking on, the matter was over before I knew it had begun, and I think that the wits of all present were similarly outrun, for an age seemed to elapse before Ellis started forward with a yell, and a hand to his hip.

“Don’t be a fool,” said Mansel, looking along his barrel into Ellis’ eyes. And then: “Put up your hands.”

I thought the fellow would have fallen down in a fit, for all the blood in his body seemed to go into his face, which grew more black then red, and he put a hand to his throat as though he were choking.

===Chinese===
没等哪个人喊出声、哪支枪打响，曼塞尔已经把他打翻在地，而且手持手枪，紧紧贴着方才罗斯·诺布尔靠着的那棵菩提树的树干。这样快的身手，我从未见过；说实在的，如今回想这一幕，我也不能凭良心说我记得它的细目：我分明在场看着，事情却在我知道它开始之前已经完了；我想在场的各位都同样被抢了先，因为埃利斯狂吼一声、一只手扑向胯间，中间仿佛隔了整整一世。

「别犯傻，」曼塞尔说，目光顺着枪管，直射进埃利斯的眼睛。跟着便是：「举起手来。」

我看那家伙险些当场抽风栽倒：他一身的血仿佛全涌上了脸，那脸由黑转红；他一手按着喉咙，像是要噎死了。

===Original===
Rose Noble lay as he had fallen, flat on his back.

“Put up your hands,” said Mansel.

Ellis did so.

“You in the cap,” said Mansel, addressing the man who had driven, “take your seat in the car and turn her round.”

===Chinese===
罗斯·诺布尔怎么倒的还怎么躺着，直挺挺仰面朝天。

「举起手来，」曼塞尔说。

埃利斯照办了。

「戴帽子的那位，」曼塞尔朝着开车的那人说，「坐回车里去，把车头掉个向。」

===Original===
When this was done, he bade them take up Rose Noble and put him into the car. They did so. Then he called the bearers to stand by Ellis’ side.

“You three will follow the car, with your hands above your heads. Drive on.”

The car moved off, but for a moment it looked as though the three pedestrians would rebel. However, I suppose they thought better of it, for, after looking at one another, like sulky dogs, with one accord they turned, and, using what dignity they could, walked out of the courtyard and down the road. Carson and Rowley followed as far as the bend; and that was the finish of a passage which was to spoil forever my enjoyment of “strong” playacting, be it never so excellently done: for this was the real thing, and to this day the bare remembrance of the affair will quicken the beating of my heart and set my nerves tingling.

===Chinese===
这一着办妥，他又叫他们抬起罗斯·诺布尔，放进车里。他们照办了。随后他叫那两个抬人的站到埃利斯身边。

「你们三个跟在车后走，双手举过头顶。开车。」

汽车开动了；一时间，那三个步行的倒像要抗命。不过我看他们是掂量过了：三人互相望了一眼，像怄气的狗一般，不约而同转过身，摆出仅剩的几分体面，出了院子，下了大路。卡森和罗利跟到弯道为止；这一场就此收梢。这一幕从此永远败坏了我对「硬汉戏」的胃口，哪怕演得再好也没用：因为这是真刀真枪；直到今天，这桩事哪怕只是空想一下，也叫我心跳加急、神经发紧。

===Original===
Here let me say that Mansel was a good deal troubled about Rose Noble, fearing that the blow he had dealt him might prove fatal, for, as I afterwards learned, he had been a famous boxer, but had long abandoned the sport for fear of killing his man.

However, as I shall show, he need have felt no concern.

Before we returned to the well, the six of us sought for points at which a man could play sentry with some success. This was by no means easy because of the woods, but, after a while, we found a ruined shrine on the top of a hill, which commanded the road for some way beyond the bend, and all that side of the estate. The shrine was about six hundred yards from the house, but there was no point nearer one half so valuable. With a sentinel there, we should be safe upon three sides, for to our north lay the river, and from the shrine you could watch the east and south: but the west was the devil. Search as we would, we could find no point at all whence the eye could observe so much as a third of the ground, and I think it would have taken four sentries to make that side secure. We had, therefore, perforce to be content with the hilltop beyond the great well. We did no more than settle these points that afternoon, nor did we visit them again the next day, but thereafter, from dawn to sunset, they were to be regularly occupied. This, to our great inconvenience: but it could not be helped.

===Chinese===
这里容我说一句：曼塞尔为罗斯·诺布尔着实愁了一场，怕他那一拳打出人命；因为后来我才知道，他当年是位有名的拳击手，只因怕在拳下害了人命，早就舍了这一行。

不过，下文自见分晓：他这愁，原是白愁的。

回到井边之前，我们六个人先把能多少顶用的哨位寻了一遍。林子挡着，这很不容易；可过了些时候，我们在一座山冈顶上找到一座废弃的小神龛，从那里望得见弯道以外相当一段大路，还能望见产业的整整一侧。那神龛离宅子约六百码，近处却再没有一处，有它一半的价值。那里放上哨，我们三面便可安心，因为北面是河，从神龛又能瞭望东、南两面：西面却是鬼门关。随我们怎么找，也寻不出一处，能把那面的地面望上三分之一；我看非得四个哨，才守得住那一面。于是我们只好将就，用大井那边的山冈顶。那天下午，我们不过把这些地点定了下来，第二天也没有再去；可是打那以后，从破晓到日落，它们须轮班占定。这对我们极不方便：却也无可如何。

===Original===
We had scarce got back to the well and found that the water had risen eighteen inches in the last three hours, when the landlord of the inn arrived.

He was full of the strangers, who had stopped to ask their way at the inn and had lately returned in such disorder of mind, and was plainly agog to know their business and what was afoot.

We told him that of them we knew nothing till ten days ago, when they had stopped us in a forest with plain intent to rob. We told how we had bayed them, and how I had crippled their car, and supposed that it was sheer rage and a desire to revenge that injury which had induced the villains to dog us to Wagensburg. But, Mansel added, he imagined we had settled their hash, and, unless they were passing venomous, we should not be troubled again.

===Chinese===
我们刚回到井边，发觉三个钟头里水涨了一英尺半，客栈老板便到了。

他满脑子都是那几个外乡人：他们在客栈门前停过，问过路；方才回来时，一个个心神大乱。他明摆着一心想知道，他们打的什么主意，出的什么事。

我们告诉他，这伙人直到十天之前，我们一无所知；那天他们在一座林子里拦住我们，明摆着要打劫。我们讲了怎样把他们堵了回去，我怎样弄残了他们的车；依我们看，正是那股子狂怒、加上要报复那一下的心思，才引得这伙恶棍一路盯梢，跟到瓦根斯堡来。不过，曼塞尔补了一句：依他看，我们已经把他们收拾妥了；只要他们不至于毒到十二分，不会再遭他们搅扰。

===Original===
“All the same,” said he, “they are armed, and I’m taking no chances at all. So, when you come up to see us, come by day, for by night all men look alike, and we don’t want to hurt our friends.”

The landlord seemed perturbed at our tale, because, while Ellis and the driver had presently left in the car, the other malefactors were proposing to stay at his inn. Rose Noble, who was still unconscious, had been carried upstairs to bed, and, though no German had been spoken, the others had made him know that they must have lodging and food. They had not asked for a doctor, and seemed untroubled by the condition of their friend: except for one battered suitcase, they had no luggage: their manner was overbearing and such as might be expected of lawless men.

We purposely offered him cold comfort, and, such was his agitation to think that he had been saddled with such undesirable guests, that the poor man displayed little interest in what he had come to see, and, merely inquiring what headway we were making against the springs, abstractedly accepted an order for supplies and set off on his way back to Lerai, like a man in an ugly dream.

===Chinese===
「话虽如此，」他说，「他们带着家伙，我是一点儿险也不冒的。所以，诸位上来看我们，请白天来；夜里人人的模样都一个样，我们可不想错伤了自己的朋友。」

听了这段话，老板显得心神不定：埃利斯和司机随即开车走了，其余几个歹人却打算在他店里住下。罗斯·诺布尔一直人事不省，已抬上楼去安置；他们虽没说过一句德语，却已经让他明白，吃住是非有不可的。他们没请大夫，对那位朋友的伤势似乎全不挂心：除了一只砸瘪的手提箱，他们别无行李：那副做派专横霸道，正是目无法纪之徒的样子。

我们有意给他泼冷水；一想到自己背上了这样一批不受欢迎的房客，他心乱如麻，特地来看的东西竟提不起一点兴致，只问了一句我们对付泉水的进境如何，便心不在焉地接下一张采买单，动身回勒拉去，恍恍惚惚，像坠在一场噩梦里。

===Original===
By sundown we had taken another seventeen feet of water out of the well.

We were so much exhausted with our labour that not one of us was fit to descend, but we were all highly pleased to think that our net gain that day had been twenty-three feet and a half, and that now but thirty-nine feet of water remained in the well. Indeed, though no one said so, I believe each hoped in his heart that by the evening of the next day we should discover the shaft.

I suppose, that in view of our progress, it was natural to nurse such hopes, for, though we knew that the water would rise in the night, we had so far no knowledge of the well beyond that it had a reputation which had never been determinedly attacked; but our chagrin in the morning was the more bitter, and it was when we pulled up our measuring-line in the grey of the dawn that for the first time we knew that, when the Count committed his two leather bags to the well, he made them wards of a Court which respected no man, which just and unjust alike might seek to move in vain.

===Chinese===
到日落时分，我们已又从井里提走了十七英尺深的水。

这一番劳作把我们累得脱了形，谁也不再有下井的气力；可想到这一天净赚了二十三英尺半，井里只剩三十九英尺深的水，人人都乐得什么似的。说实话，虽然谁也没开口，我相信各人心里都巴望，到第二天傍晚，我们便能寻见那口竖井。

我想，就我们的进境而论，抱这样的指望原是自然的，因为尽管我们晓得夜里水要回涨，这口井我们至今所知，无非是它素有难攻之名，却从未被人认真攻打过；可正因如此，次日清晨的懊丧便加倍地苦；直到熹微的晨光里我们拉起测深绳，我们才头一回明白：伯爵把那两只皮袋托付给这口井时，是把它们交给了法庭看护；这法庭不认得任何人，无论行善作恶，谁想撼动，都是徒然。

===Original===
The forces of Nature were against us, and, whilst we slept, the springs had undone our labour, much as Penelope unravelled her famous web.

During the night, the water had risen no less than thirteen feet.

This was a great blow, for, though we were yet ten feet six to the good, it showed that the day before we must have passed springs which gave at a great pace, and that it was more than likely that the lower we went, the slighter would be our gain, until at length we should lose as much by night as we had won by day. In that case, the shaft would only be discovered by a furious spell of work, at the end of which, however exhausted we might be, an effort to reach the chamber would have to be instantly made, while those who did not descend must ceaselessly labour to keep the water down, and so save their fellows from being trapped.

===Chinese===
大自然的力量站在我们对立面；我们睡觉的工夫，泉水已经拆散了我们的劳绩，一如珀涅罗珀（Penelope）拆她那匹有名的织物。

一夜之间，水竟涨了整整十三英尺。

这是个沉重的打击：虽说我们还净剩十英尺六英寸，它却表明前一天我们必定闯过了几处出水极快的泉，而且多半是越往下，所得越微薄，末了夜里失去的，怕要同白天挣来的一样多。果真如此，就只有拼一场狠活才找得到竖井；干完这阵，无论多么筋疲力尽，也须立时奋力冲进石室；而不下井的人，则须不停劳作把水位压住，好叫井下的弟兄不致被困。

===Original===
Now, this was all conjecture, to which, I fancy, the dreariness of the hour and a threatening sky made generous godmothers, but there was no blinking the facts that our supper and a short night’s rest had proved extremely expensive, and that, without a sufficiency of food and sleep, we should never be able to counter the activity of the springs.

That any of the thieves would return to trouble us this day seemed so improbable that we took no precautions beyond keeping a servant in the house, and, except that Hanbury and Carson spent an hour laying wire to the west of the castle, to complete our system, we were five to fight the water all day long.

When, half an hour after sunset, the last bucketful was pulled up, there were only nineteen feet of water left in the well.

===Chinese===
这些全是猜想；我猜，此刻的凄清、满天险恶的云，正慷慨地做着它们的教母。可有些事实不容回避：一顿晚饭加一夜短觉，代价竟大得惊人；没有足够的吃食和睡眠，我们永远斗不过泉水的勤快。

那伙贼人当天还会回来搅扰，似乎绝无可能，因此我们除了留一个仆人守屋，再没做什么防备；汉伯里和卡森花了一个钟头，在堡子西面接线，把整套系统补全；此外，五个人整日只顾同水搏斗。

日落过后半个钟头，最后一桶水提出井口，井里只剩十九英尺深的水了。

===Original===
Had it been possible, we would have returned after supper and made one mighty effort to reach the shaft, but, though Mansel and Carson and I could, I believe, have continued, George Hanbury and Rowley and Bell could hardly stand for fatigue, and would, I think, have fallen asleep at their work: and, since to ask men so weary to play sentry would have been waste of breath, there was nothing to do but look forward to the following day.

Mansel, however, consented to my going down the well, to see what was to be seen, and locate, if I could, with a bar the mouth of the shaft.

Carson had made a small seat, like that of a swing; and this was made fast to the chain. Beneath the seat was a hook, and on this we hung the lamp: the bar we lashed so that it dangled below, just clear of my feet. Then I put on a coat and a lifeline, and they let me down.

===Chinese===
倘若有法子，我们本想饭后回到井边，作最后一搏，把竖井够到；可是，尽管我相信曼塞尔、卡森和我还撑得住，乔治·汉伯里和罗利、贝尔却已累得站立不稳，我看干着活儿都要睡着了；而要叫这样疲惫的人再去站哨，无异于白费口舌：除了巴望明天，再无别的办法。

不过，曼塞尔准许我下井一趟，看看井里有什么可看的，若能行，用一根铁条探一探竖井口的位置。

卡森做了一只小座椅，像秋千座似的，拴牢在链条上；座椅底下有一只钩子，灯就挂在那上头：铁条捆在下头晃着，离我的脚尖刚好不碰。于是我穿上外套，系好保险绳，他们放我下去。

===Original===
The journey seemed unending, and I soon unhooked the lamp and looked about me whilst I was going down.

The condition of the masonry was as perfect as it had been above, but between most of the edges of the stones a thin blade of a knife would have passed, and this, I suppose, meant that they had been laid without cement to suffer the entry of the springs. That these were active was manifest, for fifty feet down the walls were running with water; but there was no gush anywhere, and where the great springs rose I could not tell. When I came to the pool, it was troubled on every side, yet so faintly that, had I not already known what to expect, I would not have believed that so unobtrusive an industry could have been so swift and masterful.

I then hung up the searchlight, and took hold of the bar, and, signalling them to lower me till the water was over my knees, began to seek the shaft with all my might. But everything was against me. The bar was too short and too heavy: the water seemed like treacle to my weary arms; if I leaned to one side, my seat swung at once to the other, as though it would cast me out. At last, by rocking myself to and fro, I managed to sound every side for about three feet; but I could do no better and, when I had almost lost my seat for the second time, I took a last look round and gave the signal for the others to pull me up.

===Chinese===
这一路像没有尽头；我不多时就摘下灯，一边下落，一边四下张望。

石工的完好与上头无异，可大多石缝之间，薄薄的刀刃也插得进去；我想这意味筑井时未用灰浆，有意让泉水渗入。这些泉活力如何，一望而知：下到五十英尺，四壁都在淌水；可哪里也没有喷涌，大泉究竟从何处涌出，我说不上来。到了水潭，四面都在起波纹，却极轻微；若非早知要来的是什么，我不会相信这样不动声色的勤勉，竟能如此神速、如此老练。

随后我挂起探照灯，抓住铁条，打手势叫他们把我再往下放，放到水没过膝盖，便用尽全身力气探寻竖井。可样样都跟我作对：铁条太短又太沉；水对我疲惫的胳膊像糖蜜一样黏；我往一侧斜，座椅立刻荡向另一侧，仿佛要把我甩出去。末了，我前后摇晃着身子，总算把每一边都探了约三英尺；再好也做不到了；待到第二次险些摔出座椅，我朝四下里看了最后一眼，打手势叫他们把我拉上去。

===Original===
Now, I had looked to see if the niches I had found in the sides the day before ran all the way down the well: and I had found that they did so. But not until I was rising did it occur to me that, as the niches had been used, so they could serve again, and that the value of a stage, however rough, from which to search for the shaft or conduct any operations would be inestimable. Before, therefore, they landed me, I begged Mansel to send for a lath with which I might measure how long the beams must be; and, after a little, he let me have my way.

The measurement took some time, for, remembering how nearly I had twice lost my seat, I dared make no movement at all except with my hands, and Mansel and Carson had to hold me close to the wall. Then I could not see, until they had fixed the light, and twice the lath had to be returned and sawn to another length. However, at last it was over, and I was pulled up. And, after supper, that night Carson and I cut two rafters out of an outhouse roof; and, since of the wood in the stables three planks remained, before we lay down to sleep we had our stage.

===Chinese===
对了，我原是特意察看前一天在井壁上发现的那些壁龛，看它们是否直通井底：结果表明它们一直通到底。可直到往上起时，我才想起：壁龛当初既然用得上，如今就还能再用；有一座平台——哪怕再简陋——好站在上面探寻竖井或做任何操作，其价值无可估量。于是，在他们把我送回地面之前，我求曼塞尔差人取一条板条来，量一量木梁该有多长；过了一会儿，他依了我。

这一量费了不少工夫：想到自己两回险些摔出座椅，我除了一双手，哪里都不敢动，曼塞尔和卡森只得把我抵在井壁上。随后我又看不见了，直到他们把灯固定好；那板条还两回收回去，锯成别的长度。可是总算量完，我也被拉了上来。晚饭后，那天夜里我和卡森从一间外屋的房顶拆下两根椽子；马厩里的木料还剩三块板，于是躺下睡觉之前，我们的平台已经有了。

## VIII The Race for the Chamber / VIII 争夺石室

===Original===

The next morning we held a short council.

The system of reliefs, which had served us so long and so well, was now a thing of the past: and we were all agreed that, once we had closed our bases, except to communicate with Carson or to go out as a spy, no one must leave the dungeon until the treasure was won.

That the stranger whom Hanbury had seen was the tanner’s brother we had no doubt, and, to judge from the fellow’s demeanour, so far as we were concerned, the two were at one with the thieves: if this were so, since they were the innkeeper’s allies, our enemies now numbered eight, instead of five.

Yet, neither of these conclusions troubled us near so much as the thought that Rose Noble must know that there was a second entrance into the oubliette. This was a serious matter: for, now that he knew, he would not rest till he had found it, and, as if that were not enough, we had left him a definite clue. Rowley had covered our tracks as best he could, but he could not repair the door which Mansel had forced: its broken condition the thieves were bound to remark, and, the instant they saw it, they would know that the entrance they sought led out of the castle itself. “And so,” said Mansel, “it’s only a matter of time. It may take them a day or an hour: but in the end they’ll find it, and then the balloon will go up.”

===Chinese===

第二天早晨，我们开了一个简短的议事会。

沿用既久、出力甚多的换班制度，如今已成过去；我们一致议定：各处基地一撤，除了同卡森（Carson）通消息，或是出去做探子，财宝不到手，谁也不许离开地牢。

汉伯里（Hanbury）看见的那个生人，必是制革匠的兄弟，这一点我们毫不怀疑；看那家伙的做派，凡涉及我们，他们两个跟盗贼早是一伙的；果真如此，他们既是客栈老板的帮手，我们的仇敌便从五个变成了八个。

可这两条结论合在一起，搅扰我们的程度，也不及另一层念头的一半：罗斯·诺布尔（Rose Noble）必定已经知道，囚井（oubliette）还有一个入口。这可是桩大事：他既已知道，不找到它便决不罢休；何况犹恐不足似的，我们又给他留下了一条确凿的线索。罗利（Rowley）把我们的痕迹能遮掩的都遮掩了，可曼塞尔（Mansel）撬坏的那扇门，他修不回来：门那副破败相，盗贼们必定看得出来；一看见，他们便知道，要找的那个入口，原来就通在城堡自己身上。「所以，」曼塞尔说，「这只是个时间问题。兴许要一天，兴许只要一个钟头；可到头来他们总会找到，到那时，大祸就要临头了。」

===Original===

Now, if we guarded the trap, though they found it, to force an entry that way might be very hard, for three men armed, in the darkness, could hold it against an army, similarly equipped. But what a man has done once, he will do again; and we had little doubt that, if we opposed their entry, they would at once resort to the use of that terrible weapon which they had already employed, that is to say, of bombs: and stand up to these we could not, if for no other reason, because against such an attack no man born of woman could hope to hold such a place. We could bar the postern of nights, we could set an electric alarm above the stile, we could keep watch: but more we could not do, and must try the fortune of war when the moment came. But Mansel decided that in future we must always go armed, even though we were working at the nose of the shaft.

It was then arranged that the next morning I should go to the culvert, and, taking the Rolls from Carson, send him back to the dungeon and myself drive to St. Martin for the last time.

“You see,” said Mansel, “at the moment we’re not up against Time: but I shouldn’t be surprised if we were, before the curtain comes down. Give me five minutes’ start, and I’ll be at Calais five hours before Rose Noble: but not if I’ve got to trip round, paying my bills. So we’ll do three more reliefs. When you’ve bade farewell to St. Martin, Hanbury can visit Villach; and then I can go to Salzburg and make all smooth and clear for us to withdraw. I think we should each go alone, because four’s none too many to hold the fort. And I think we’ll have Tester here: he’ll rather like this place, and he’ll be devilish useful, watching that trap.”

With that we talked no more, but went to work, Mansel and Hanbury to setting the electric alarm, and Rowley and Bell and I to driving the shaft.

===Chinese===

话说回来，活板门我们守着，纵然叫他们找着，想从那里破门而入也难得狠：黑暗里三个带枪的人，对上同样装备的一支军队也守得住。可人干过一回的事，总会再干一回；他们若进不来，会不会立时又祭出那件已经用过的可怕兵器——炸弹——我们心里没有多少疑问。而这东西我们顶不住——别的不算，单凭一条：这样的打法面前，娘胎里生出来的人，谁也休想守住这么个地方。我们可以每夜闩死边门，可以在梯级上方安一具电警铃（electric alarm），可以派人守望：可再多的便没有了，只好到那一刻，听凭战事的运气。不过曼塞尔拿定主意：往后我们永远随身带枪，哪怕在坑道头（the nose of the shaft）干活也是如此。

当下议定：第二天早晨我去涵洞，从卡森手里接过劳斯莱斯（Rolls），打发他回地牢，我自己则最后一次驱车去圣马丁（St. Martin）。

「你们瞧，」曼塞尔说，「这会儿我们还没有同时间对垒；可幕落之前，保不齐就要对垒上了。给我五分钟的先手，我能比罗斯·诺布尔早五个钟头到加来（Calais）；可若是我还得满处转着去结账，那就免谈。所以我们再做三班换班。你同圣马丁辞了行，汉伯里去一趟菲拉赫（Villach）；随后我去萨尔茨堡（Salzburg），把我们撤退的事，铺排得干干净净。我想咱们各人独自去，因为守营盘，四个已不算多。泰斯特（Tester）嘛，我想留在这儿：这地方他会喜欢；看住那扇活板门，他管用得很。」

说定之后，我们不再多话，各自去干活：曼塞尔和汉伯里去安设电警铃，罗利、贝尔（Bell）和我去掘坑道。

===Original===

Thirteen days had gone by since we had altered our course, and so well had we wrought in this time that but twenty-three yards lay between us and what we had come to call “live ground.” We gave it that name, because, though we knew it was surrounding the chamber, as a garden a house, we were far less certain where within it the chamber lay. We hoped and believed that, by holding straight on through this region, we should strike some part of the chamber before we had gone five yards: we were equally sure that, if we had not found it in nine, this would be because we had passed it; and, in such an event, we proposed to go no further, but to drive a new shaft at right angles through the middle of the live ground. We had made up our minds that this region was not more than nine yards square, and we steadfastly refused to consider that somewhere within its area the chamber did not lie.

Whether the progress we made was actually swift or slow I cannot fairly pronounce, for how six sextons would have fared, had they been set such a task, I cannot say. But what we lacked in experience, I think we made up in zeal: and, after all, digging is a simple, downright business, which one man, if he will, can soon do as well as another. Be that as it may, I do not think any men could have laboured more heartily than we had, since our brush with the tanner upon the ninth night of June. But now, since our life was to be less easy, we determined to cut our confinement as short as ever we could and to that end to redouble our efforts to reach our goal. We had not much hope of so doing before the thieves discovered the trap: but there was a chance that we might, and, faint though it was, this certainly spurred us on, for, although we were ready enough to show our teeth, once such a battle was joined, no one could possibly tell what the outcome would be.

And there, of course, the thieves had a valuable advantage: for, if one of them was wounded, the others would let him lie or die for want of attention, rather than care for him to the cost of their enterprise: but, if one of us was hurt and needed a doctor’s aid, everything else, of course, would go by the board.

In this sense the dice were loaded: and, though hitherto we had been most fortunate, this spectre always haunted each passage of arms that we had.

Full of these thoughts, we worked as hard as we could, and by the end of that day had actually advanced five yards, so that but eighteen remained between us and the live ground.

===Chinese===

自改换方向以来，十三天过去了；这段工夫我们干得着实漂亮，离我们所谓的「活土」（live ground）只剩二十三码。我们给它起这个名字，因为，虽说我们知道它环抱着石室，如园子环着房子，石室在它中间的哪一处，我们却远没有那么有把握。我们盼望、也相信，照直穿过这一带，不出五码便会撞上石室的某一面；我们同样确信，九码之内若还找不着，那必定是我们把它掘过去了；果真如此，我们打算就此打住，改从活土正中，横着再开一条新坑道。我们认定这一带至多九码见方，并且一口咬定：它的范围之内，石室断没有不在的道理。

我们干得究竟算快算慢，我不敢妄断；把这样的差事派给六个掘墓人，他们会干成什么样，我说不上来。可经验里欠下的，我想，热忱补上了；再说，挖土本是一桩简单、直截了当的营生，只要肯下力气，一个人很快便能同另一个人干得一样好。且不论如何，自六月九日夜里同制革匠交手以来，我不信有谁干得比我们更起劲。只是如今，日子要过得不再轻松了，我们便打定主意，把这囚居的日子剪得越短越好，为此加倍卖力，朝目标赶去。在盗贼发现活板门之前便大功告成，指望不大；可机会总有一线，机会虽薄，却实实在在鞭策着我们；因为，我们固然随时龇得出牙齿来，可这样的仗一旦接火，谁也说不准结局如何。

这一点上，盗贼们天然占着便宜：他们中间谁挂了彩，旁人宁可让他躺着、没人照应地死掉，也不肯为照顾他而坏了自己的大计；可我们要是有一个人伤了、需要医生诊治，别的一切，自然都只好撇下。

从这个意义上说，骰子是灌了铅的：虽说迄今我们运气极好，这道魔影却始终在我们每一场交锋里出没。

怀着这些念头，我们拼命地干；到那天收工，竟推进了五码，离活土只剩十八码。

===Original===

At one o’clock the next morning I left for the culvert, charged by Mansel to swim down the middle of the river and not to attempt to land for a quarter of a mile. I was then to strike across country and by no means to use the road, until I had left the junction two miles behind. Directly I had found Carson I was to send him back, for else it would be broad daylight before he could reach the shoot. “Indeed,” said Mansel, “I’m banking on your meeting by half past two, for Carson is sure to be early and so, I think, are you, for the culvert is less than four miles as the crow flies. Tell him on no account to let Tester off the lead and to hold the end in his mouth as he swims to the shoot.”

I was at the culvert at half past two and had just found the note which Carson had lodged in the brickwork under the arch, when I heard a step above me, and there he was.

Two minutes later he and Tester were gone, and I was turning the Rolls.

There was little to do at St. Martin, except to collect my belongings and pay my bill. But I had to explain how it was that I came a day late and alone and without the basket which my hostess had so often put up. I might have found this hard; but, between their relief to see me and their distress to learn that I should return no more, the good people seemed to have no room left for surprise. Indeed, I might have been a fugitive monarch, resting at the house of a loyal before resuming his flight, for they could not do enough to show their esteem and goodwill. And, when at last I left them, I had to give them my word that I would one day come back, and, even so, enough honest tears were shed to make me feel ashamed that I had imposed so much on their simplicity.

Hanbury met me at the culvert, but had nothing at all to report, save that another five yards had been added to the length of the shaft. And, by the time he was back on the following day, we were within eight yards of the live ground.

===Chinese===

第二天凌晨一点，我动身去涵洞，曼塞尔嘱咐我顺河心游下去，四分之一英里之内不许试图上岸；随后横穿野地，断断不许走大路，直到把汇流处（the junction）甩下两英里。一寻见卡森，我即刻打发他回去，不然等他赶到溜槽，天已大亮。「说真的，」曼塞尔说，「我指望你们两点半碰头：卡森必定早到，你我想也不会晚，因为涵洞直线不足四英里。跟他说，无论如何不许松开泰斯特的牵绳，叫他游向溜槽的时候，把绳头叼在嘴里。」

两点半，我到了涵洞；刚在桥拱下的砖缝里寻见卡森存的条子，就听见头顶有脚步声——正是他。

两分钟后，他和泰斯特走了，我掉转了劳斯莱斯。

在圣马丁，除了收拾行李、结清账目，没什么事可做。可我不得不解释：为什么我迟了一天、独自一个人，还没带着女主人时常替我打点的那只食篮。这本不好开口；可好心的人们，一见我回来，宽慰还来不及，再听说我从此不来了，又难过起来，两下里一挤，竟没有留下惊讶的余地。说实在的，我简直像一位亡命的君王，在忠顺之臣家里歇脚，再继续奔逃；因为他们表达敬重与善意，只恨法子不多。末了临别，我不得不许诺改日一定回来；即便如此，他们淌下的老实的眼泪也够多了，叫我为自己这样欺负老实人感到羞惭。

汉伯里到涵洞来接我，可什么也没得报，只说坑道又长了五码。到第二天他回来，我们离活土已不足八码。

===Original===

We were all glad of Tester: and Tester was glad of us. He soon understood that, when there was no one to spare, he was expected to guard the oubliette, and I think he enjoyed wandering about in the darkness﻿—for now we burned no lights, except in the gallery and at the nose of the shaft﻿—and conducting an endless scrutiny of the great banks of earth. The shoot seemed to fascinate him, and he always contrived to be present, when its flap was withdrawn. I fancy he thought that it must have been made by some beast and was continually hoping that its creator would emerge.

When Carson took his leave, I could not help wondering when we should see him again: for, with Mansel’s return, the last of our reliefs would be over, and, if all went well, the next time we crossed the river would be the last. He was very loth to leave us and constantly lamented that he had not killed Rose Noble on the night when we visited the well. “One minute more,” he would say, “and I should have had him cold. And that would have saved us a peck of trouble, sir; for, if ever I saw one, there goes a dangerous man.”

Be sure I agreed with him. And yet I must honestly confess that, if we had suddenly learned that the thieves had withdrawn, the game would have lost a curiously attractive savour, which even the lifting of the treasure would not have wholly restored. For danger, once you have tasted it, is a superlative spice.

If Carson was loth to go, we were sorry enough to lose him, for he was a fine workman and a first-class shot. What was more, he had the brain of a fighter and, though there might be no one to lead him, could be trusted to think for himself and to hold his own.

However, neither Rowley nor Bell could manage a car as could he; besides which, it was good to know that our line of retreat was held by such capable hands.

===Chinese===

我们个个喜欢泰斯特，泰斯特也喜欢我们。他很快就明白了：没有多余的人手时，看守囚井便是他的差事；我想，他在黑暗里游荡是快活的——如今除了画廊和坑道头，我们已不点灯——他把那一座座大土堆检视个没完。溜槽似乎叫他着迷；活板一掀，他总有法子到场。我猜他认定那必是哪头野兽弄出来的，时时刻刻盼着造它的东西钻出来。

卡森告辞的时候，我忍不住揣想：几时才能再见他？因为曼塞尔一回来，我们的换班便到头了；一切顺利的话，下一回渡河便是最后一回。他老大不愿意离开我们，老是嗟叹探井那一夜没能结果了罗斯·诺布尔。「再多一分钟，」他总说，「我就把他撂倒，叫他凉透了。那样能省下好一摊麻烦，先生；要说世上有危险人物，那位便是一个。」

我当然跟他一心。可我必须老实承认：倘若忽然听说盗贼们撤了，这场游戏便会失掉一股说不出的诱人的滋味——纵然财宝到手，也补不全。危险这种佐料，一旦尝过，便是顶了天的美味。

卡森不愿走，我们失掉他，也实在难过：他活儿干得漂亮，又是个头一等的好枪手。更难得的是他长着一颗战士的脑子；纵然没人指挥，也信得过他自会拿主意，守得住自己的地步。

只是，罗利和贝尔都开不来他那样的车；再者，退路握在这样能干的手里，也是一桩安心事。

===Original===

His instructions were clear.

So soon as he reached Salzburg he was to lay up the Rolls and, taking the second car, drive her to Villach. There he was to bestow her at the inn which Hanbury had used, and then to return by train in search of the Rolls. After that he was to go no more to Salzburg, but to take up his quarters at Villach, holding both cars in condition to leave at an instant’s notice by day or night. Each night at eleven o’clock he was to be at the culvert and, having concealed the Rolls, to wait there till twelve. He was then to walk to a point a mile and a half away, where seven trees grew together on the top of a hill. From here could be seen the castle and a slice of the river below. If he saw nothing unusual, he was to go his way: if he saw one window lighted, he was to return to the culvert and wait till we came: if he saw two illumined, he was to leave the Rolls and come to the shoot: but, if all three were aglow, he was to get the Rolls and drive down, past the junction and on to the Lerai road.

We had little to report to Mansel, beyond that our progress had been slow. For, since he had left, we had only advanced three yards, because we had encountered a boulder as big as a sheep, the dislodgment and disposal of which had taken the best part of five hours. But, if, instead of progressing, we had actually retired, I do not think Mansel would have cared, because he was so much relieved to find that the secret of the trap was still inviolate and that we had not, in his absence, been called upon to fight for our lives.

===Chinese===

给他的指令清清楚楚。

他一进萨尔茨堡，便把劳斯莱斯寄存起来，换上第二辆车，开去菲拉赫。到了那边，把车安顿在汉伯里住过的那家客栈，随后坐火车回来寻劳斯莱斯。此后他不再去萨尔茨堡，就在菲拉赫住定，把两辆车都拾掇得随时可以昼夜启程。每晚十一点，他到涵洞，把劳斯莱斯藏好，在那儿守到十二点。随后步行到一英里半外一处地方——那里，一座山顶上七棵树长作一丛。从那里望得见城堡，和底下一幅河面。没见什么异样，他自管回去；望见一扇窗亮着，便回涵洞，守到我们来；两扇亮着，便撇下劳斯莱斯，到溜槽去；三扇全亮，便取了劳斯莱斯开下山去，驶过汇流处，直上勒拉（Lerai）的大路。

给曼塞尔可报的不多，只一句：进度慢了。因为他走后我们才进了三码——撞上一块绵羊大的顽石，把它撬松、安置妥当，耗去了五个钟头的大半。可就算我们非但没进反而后退，我想曼塞尔也不会放在心上；活板门的秘密分毫无损，他不在的这些天我们也没被迫为性命而战——这叫他宽慰极了。

===Original===

That day we made a great effort; and by eight o’clock that evening we had reached the coveted region and had broken the live ground.

Our excitement now began to run high, and I was for bolting my supper, in order to get back to work: but Mansel said he should like to visit the combe, and me to go with him.

“It may seem idle,” he said: “but those who don’t look don’t see. To visit the well or the castle would take more time than I think we can fairly spare: but to go to the combe and back will take us less than an hour. I hope and believe it will be our last reconnaissance, but I think we ought to make it before we take off our coats for the final push.”

And so we went out together, when it was dark.

Hanbury told me later that, when we returned, Mansel was rather pale, but I looked an older man. I have no doubt I did: for, though we had seen no one and heard no sound, what we had found was enough to age any man who thinks the fruits of his labour worth taking up.

*At the foot of the gutter we had found a miniature lake.*

===Chinese===

那一天我们铆足了劲；到傍晚八点，我们进到那片朝思暮想的地界，破开了活土。

这一来我们的兴奋开始奔涌；我本想囫囵吞了晚饭，好回去接着干活：可曼塞尔说，他想去谷地（the combe）一趟，要我同去。

「看着像是多余，」他说，「可不去看的，什么也看不见。去井边或城堡，费的工夫，我看我们匀不出来；去谷地打个来回，却用不了一个钟头。我巴望、也相信，这是最后一回侦察了；可我想，脱下外衣做最后一冲之前，这一趟总该走。」

于是天黑之后，我们一同出了门。

汉伯里后来告诉我：我们回来的时候，曼塞尔面色颇白，而我看去老了一截。我想是不会错的；因为虽说没见一个人影、没听一点声响，我们发现的东西，也够叫任何一个觉得自己劳动的果实值得收取的人凭空添上几岁年纪。

*在水槽（the gutter）的尽头，我们发现了一片小小的湖。*

---

===Original===

Now, whilst we were drawing water, we had never gone down to see the pool we had made, and, though we all remembered the sodden condition of the ground on the day I was taken by Ellis, that was nothing to go by, for then no water had been drawn for eighteen or twenty hours. We had, therefore, the bare fact before us that the enemy was making for the first time a truly formidable effort to empty the well﻿—a feat which we had accomplished in less than three days. How long they had been at work, how much water they had drawn, how the results of their labour were comparing with those of ours we could not so much as guess: *but what six men had done eight men could do*; and this indisputable verity, sternly illustrated by the lake at the foot of the combe, bade fair to sap the resolution from which our endeavours sprang.

I can only speak for myself, but I know that, when I was back in the gallery, I felt tired and was glad to sit down and that, when Bell brought me dry shoes, the business of changing seemed a burden and I told him to put them down and let me be.

Then I heard Mansel laugh.

===Chinese===

话要说回来：抽水的时候，我们从不曾下去看看自己造成的那口潭；而虽说我们全记得我被埃利斯捉去那天地上的烂泥相，那却作不得准，因为当时已有十八九个钟头不曾抽水了。摆在我们眼前的，只剩一条干巴巴的事实：敌人有生以来头一回，为排空这口井使上了真正可怕的力气——这桩功业，我们不出三天便告成功。他们干了多久，抽了多少水，他们的成绩同我们的比较起来如何，我们连猜也无从猜起：*可六个人办得到的事，八个人也办得到*；这条无可辩驳的真理，由谷地脚下那面湖作了严峻的注脚，眼看要把滋生出我们一切努力的决心，一寸寸蛀蚀掉。

我只能说我自己：回到画廊，我只觉乏，坐下来才好受些；贝尔给我拿来干鞋，换鞋这件小事竟成了负担，我叫他撂下，由我去。

随后我听见曼塞尔笑了。

===Original===

“Rose Noble leads,” he said lightly. “And yet I’ll back the old firm. There’s the straight to be covered yet, and I fancy they’re full of running. Besides, I don’t know how you fellows feel, but I haven’t shovelled dirt for six weeks for the pleasure of watching Rose Noble pick my peach. Then again, the game’s the thing.

“And now let’s see where we stand.

“As yet they’ve not got to the shaft. That is clear, for that water was drawn today, but the gutter was dry: which means they’ve stopped work for the night. If it was over, and they’d found, there wouldn’t be so much water at the foot of the combe, for once they’ve uncovered the shaft, they’ve only to bail to keep the water down: and five hours’ bailing wouldn’t make all that mess.

“Again, I know they’re eight: but only four will draw water as fast as we: and one of those﻿—Rose Noble﻿—has something better to do, for you can’t command such a rabble and bear a hand as well. Besides, I’ll stake my oath that, water to draw or no, Rose Noble’s still trying to find the way to the oubliette. And I think it more than likely that they’re keeping an eye on the shoot.

“And last of all, they none of them know of the bars. And that’s a fruit of a thought, for I’ll lay my soul to a cesspool that, apart from anything else, they’re short of a file.”

===Chinese===

「领跑的是罗斯·诺布尔，」他轻描淡写地说，「可我还是押老字号。好歹还有一段直道要赶，我看他们后劲十足。再说，你们几位怎么想，我不知道；我铲了六个礼拜的土，可不是为着看罗斯·诺布尔来摘我的桃子。再说一句，游戏本身才是要紧的。

「好了，咱们看看眼下的形势。

「这会儿他们还没够到竖井。这看得分明：那片水是今天抽的，可水槽是干的——就是说，他们今夜已经收了工。要是活儿已经完事、宝也到了手，谷地脚下便不会有这么些水；因为竖井一露头，他们只消戽水，就能把水面压住：戽五个钟头，弄不出这么一大摊。

「再说，我知道他们是八个：可抽起水来赶得上我们的，至多四个；而这四个里的一个——罗斯·诺布尔——另有更要紧的事，因为一面统带这班乌合之众、一面还动手帮衬，是办不到的。况且我敢起誓：不管有水没水要抽，罗斯·诺布尔还在想法子找去囚井的路。还有一层，他们多半正盯着溜槽呢。

「末了顶要紧的一条：铁条的事，他们一概不知。这是念头结出来的果子；因为我敢拿灵魂同一个污水坑打赌：且不说别的，他们手里准短着一把锉刀。」

===Original===

With that, he ordered Rowley to open a case of champagne, “for, though,” said he, “I meant us to drink it when the treasure had been won, now that I come to think, we might not have time: and it seems a pity to leave it for Ellis and Rose.”

I can relate his words, but I cannot describe the way in which he spoke them, or the quiet, confident manner with which he seemed to be putting Misfortune to shame.

Enough that he lifted us up and carried us all away.

And, since neither Hanbury nor I would wait for the wine, Mansel himself brought a bottle to the nose of the shaft and made us drink.

We worked straight through that night, and it was eight the next morning before Mansel called a halt. By then we had tunnelled four yards into the live ground. This had meant working at a tremendous pace, for we faithfully maintained its dimensions and propped the shaft as we went. The heat was awful, and the want of air most vile: but we had grown used to these conditions, from which we had begun to suffer as soon as we turned the shaft. Still, we came down into the gallery, swaying like drunken men, and Hanbury and Bell fell asleep over their food.

===Chinese===

说着，他吩咐罗利开一箱香槟，「因为，」他说，「我本打算等财宝到手再喝；可转念一想，到那时只怕没有工夫了：留给埃利斯和罗斯，未免可惜。」

他说的话我记了下来；可他说话的神气——那份安静、笃定、仿佛把厄运奚落得抬不起头来的做派——我形容不出。

只消说一句：他把我们托举起来，把我们全都带走了。

而且，汉伯里和我都不肯等酒，曼塞尔便亲手把一瓶拿到坑道头，硬要我们喝下去。

那一夜我们一口气干到底；到第二天早晨八点，曼塞尔才喊停。那时我们已在活土里掘进四码。这意味着要以惊人的速度干活；因为我们一丝不苟地守着坑道的尺寸，随掘随撑。热得可怕，缺气最是难堪；这些苦头，打从坑道拐弯那天起我们就开始受，如今早已惯了。然而下到画廊的时候，我们还是像醉汉一样摇摇晃晃；汉伯里和贝尔吃着吃着就睡着了。

===Original===

Then Mansel told Rowley and me to take our rest and said that he should take Tester and sleep in the oubliette. I believe he said something else, for I remember laughing, but before my laughter was over I was asleep.

Now how long Mansel slept I do not know, but he woke us at noon, to say that he had sunk a crowbar at the nose of the shaft and had encountered water four feet below its floor.

Except that he had found the chamber, he could not have brought us more encouraging news, for it showed that we had, at least, no vertical error to fear and that, roughly, our floor was level with that of the chamber itself.

In a moment, therefore, we were wide awake and afoot and all agog to return to the nose of the shaft. But of this Mansel would not hear, until we had all of us bathed and broken our fast.

“Method in all things,” he said. “Disorder never won yet and never will. We went off the deep end last night, but we’re not going to do it again. From now on we’ll labour by shifts: there’ll always be four at work and one at rest. I don’t know how much we can stand; but it won’t be for long, and four hours on to one off seems the most convenient rule.”

===Chinese===

随后曼塞尔叫罗利和我去歇息，说他自己带泰斯特去囚井里睡。我记得他还说了句什么，因为我笑了；可笑声未完，我已睡着了。

曼塞尔睡了多久，我不知道；可他正午叫醒了我们，说他在坑道头扎下一根撬棍，在坑道底面以下四英尺的地方碰着了水。

除非他已经找到石室，再没有比这更叫我们振奋的信儿了；因为这表明，至少垂直方向的偏差我们不必再怕，而且我们的底面，同石室的地板大致齐平。

于是转眼之间，我们个个醒了过来，一跃而起，急急乎要回坑道头去。可这个曼塞尔说什么也不依，非要我们个个先洗过澡、吃过饭不可。

「凡事都得有章法，」他说，「乱来从来没赢过，往后也决计赢不了。昨儿夜里我们干得脱了形；可不能再这么着了。打今儿起轮班干活：永远四个人在掘，一个人在歇。我们的筋骨撑得住多少，我不知道；反正长不了；干四个钟头、歇一个钟头，顶便当。」

===Original===

And, whilst we were eating, he outlined what must be done to cope with a sudden attack.

“The one who is off duty must sleep in the oubliette. Tester will wake him all right, in the event of attack. The moment he’s waked, he will withdraw to the ramp﻿—of course, taking Tester with him﻿—and close the postern gate. And he will hold the ramp, while the others will hold the shaft. In this way we shall have them between two fires, and, if we don’t loose off too soon, it ought to be a walkover.”

Then he told us to mind how we went at the head of the ramp, as well as at the mouth of the shaft, because he had built two breastworks out of some sacks of earth.

“Why, you can’t have rested at all,” cried Hanbury.

“I had two full hours,” said Mansel, “and now I’m to have one more. Chandos, what time do you make it?”

===Chinese===

吃着饭，他把一旦遇袭该怎么办的路数，勾画了一个大概。

「歇班的那一个睡在囚井里。真有袭击，泰斯特保管把他叫醒。他一醒，便退上壁垒——自然，带着泰斯特——关上边门。他守壁垒，其余的人守坑道。这样一来，敌人便夹在两股火力的中间；只要开枪开得不早，这一仗便是唾手可得。」

随后他嘱咐我们：在壁垒顶上走，同在坑道口走一样，都要留神脚下，因为他拿几袋土垒起了两座胸墙。

「嗳呀，你哪儿算歇过，」汉伯里嚷道。

「整整两个钟头，」曼塞尔说，「如今再补一个。钱多斯，你看是几点了？」

===Original===

I told him twenty past twelve.

“I shall be your relief,” said he. “I count upon you to wake me at half past one.” I suppose I hesitated, for he continued at once. “Unless you give me your word, I shall not sleep.”

“I promise,” said I.

“Very good,” said Mansel. “But if, before then, you strike oil﻿—well, I don’t mind being called early to hear the news.”

Five minutes later we were at the nose of the shaft.

===Chinese===

我说十二点二十。

「下一班换我，」他说，「我指望你一点半把我叫醒。」我大概犹豫了一下，因为他随即接着说：「你不给我句准话，我就不睡。」

「我答应，」我说。

「很好，」曼塞尔说，「不过，要是到那之前你们先挖出了油——嘿，早早叫醒我来听信儿，我不介意。」

五分钟后，我们已经回到坑道头。

===Original===

We had still five yards to go, before we should come to the end of the belt of live ground: but, though we went steadily forward, we now took to searching right and left, by dint of driving a crowbar, as though it were a great nail, into the wall. Again and again, by this means, we thought we had found the chamber, but when we had laid bare the obstruction, each time it proved to be some boulder or two or three smaller stones. In this way the tunnel began to lose its symmetry, and, though this could not be helped, the propping of the roof with timber became a less downright business and wasted a lot of time.

We worked frantically, speaking very little, but doing the best we could to maintain the system of labour which should turn to the best account such joint strength as we had.

The hewer stood on a sheet on to which his winnings must fall. As soon as these began to encumber him he would stand back and away: at once his assistant would draw back the sheet from the face and lay in its place another to catch the next fall of earth. The assistant then disposed of the soil he had drawn away, and by the time he was back the second sheet would be full. The carpenter followed the hewer as close as he could, pitching his uprights and crossbars and wedging them into place: and the fourth was man of all work, now shovelling loose earth that had fallen clear of the sheet or had not fallen, now helping the carpenter, and now bringing up fresh wood.

An hour before his relief, each man had a pint of champagne, and, when he was waked, was given a quarter of an hour in which to bathe and eat. Mansel fed Tester himself twice in the day, and that was all the attention the poor dog had: yet he was as good as gold, never obtruding himself as so many dogs would have done, but seeming to know that we were fighting with Time and faithfully keeping to his post in the oubliette.

By eleven o’clock that night we had advanced five yards and were clear of the live ground.

===Chinese===

穿过活土这一带，还差五码；可我们虽然照旧稳步向前，却开始左右探摸——把撬棍当一根大钉子，一下一下往墙上杵。靠这个法子，我们一回回以为自己摸着了石室；可把障碍物剥露出来一看，回回不过是某块顽石，或是两三块小些的石头。这么一来，坑道渐渐失去了齐整；这固然没法子，顶上的木工活却不再那么直截了当，白白耗去许多工夫。

我们发狂似的干着，话极少，却竭力维持那套劳动的章程，好叫我们合起来的力气得到最划算的用场。

掘进的人站在一幅帆布上，凿下来的土都落在这上头。一觉得碍事，他便退后躲开：助手立刻把帆布从工作面上抽走，另铺一幅新的，接住下一轮落土。助手随即将抽出的土处置掉；等他回来，第二幅帆布已经满了。木工紧紧跟着掘进工，能贴多近贴多近，竖立柱、架横撑，打上楔子敲进位：第四个人当杂差，一会儿铲那落在帆布以外的、或是压根还没落下来的松土，一会儿帮木工的忙，一会儿又往上运新木料。

交班前一个钟头，每人一品脱香槟；叫醒之后，给一刻钟洗澡吃饭。泰斯特一天两顿，由曼塞尔亲手喂——可怜的狗得到的照顾尽在于此：可他乖得像块金子，从不硬往前凑——多少狗都要凑的——倒像明白我们是在同时间抢命，忠忠耿耿守着囚井里的岗。

到那晚十一点，我们已推进五码，出了活土。

===Original===

It was impossible not to be disappointed and very hard not to be dismayed. And, when I had called for a crowbar and, with three mighty blows, slammed this up to its head into the nose of the shaft, *and touched not so much as a pebble*, I think we all avoided each other’s eyes.

But Hanbury﻿—for Mansel was resting﻿—wasted no time. By his direction we immediately withdrew five yards and began to drive a new tunnel out of the left-hand wall.

For no reason that I can offer, the half hour that followed seems for me to stand out of that period of toil and trouble; and I remember most vividly the sob of the hewer each time that he launched his pickaxe and the smell of sweat and the blinding glare of the searchlight and even a mark on a timber retaining the left-hand wall. To our right, the five yards we had won to no purpose continually mocked us, like the Psalmist’s bulls of Bashan, gaping upon us with its mouth: to our left, our long, clean-cut gallery seemed to be leading fantastically into another world. I can see Hanbury poising the compass and hear him curse as his sweat fell on to its dial: and, when Bell who was hewing, missed his stroke, I remember snatching the pickaxe and missing mine.

===Chinese===

失望是免不了的，灰心也几乎按捺不住。我叫来一根撬棍，抡了三大下，把它齐头砸进坑道头，*却连一粒碎石也没碰着*；这时我想，我们谁都躲着谁的眼睛了。

可汉伯里——因为曼塞尔在歇班——一刻也不耽搁。奉他的示下，我们立时退回五码，从左边的墙上另开一条新坑道。

说不出个道理来，随后那半个钟头，在那一整段劳苦愁烦的岁月里，于我竟独自凸立出来；我记得清清楚楚：掘进工每抡一镐时的那一声喘，汗的气味，探照灯的强光，连左边墙上一根撑木上的一个记号，都历历在目。右边，我们白费气力挣来的那五码，不断地嘲弄我们，像诗篇作者的巴珊公牛（the bulls of Bashan），张着嘴瞪视我们；左边，我们那条又长又齐整的坑道，仿佛鬼使神差，要通到另一个世界里去。我看得见汉伯里端着罗盘，听得见他咒骂，因为汗正滴在罗盘面上；抡镐的贝尔落了一空，我记得自己夺过镐来，也落了空。

===Original===

So the work went on; and the niche in the left-hand wall had grown to an entrance, when Hanbury looked at his wristwatch and cried that my time was up.

I stumbled back to wake Mansel and fall asleep in his stead, for the champagne had done its business and my knees were beginning to sag.

Almost at once Hanbury woke me, and I started up with a cry.

“Have you found?”

“Found be damned,” says George. “You’ve had five minutes over your time.”

So it was with us all: and we battled rather than laboured, fighting with nature, like madmen, in our effort to find the chamber before she could wear us down. For the pace was too hot to last: we all knew that: and unless we could win very soon, we were playing a losing game. Yet we went steadily on, like men in a dream, losing all count of Time and confusing Night with Day. Indeed, the demands of the battle so wholly possessed our senses that, used in some other direction, these were beginning to fail. We shouted, one to another, when a whisper could have been heard: the hand that could still ply a hammer, could not be trusted to raise a glass to the lips: and, if ever I glanced at my wristwatch, this seemed a great way off.

===Chinese===

活计这样接着干；左壁上的凹龛已经长成一道门洞，这时汉伯里看了看腕表，嚷道我的钟点到了。

我踉跄着回去叫醒曼塞尔，替他去睡；香槟已办完它的差事，我的膝盖开始打软。

转眼汉伯里便叫醒了我；我喊了一声，一跃而起。

「找着了？」

「找着个鬼，」乔治（George）说，「你超了五分钟。」

我们人人都到了这个地步：与其说是干活，不如说是厮杀——疯子似的同自然力搏斗，要抢在它把我们磨垮之前找到石室。这样的速度撑不了多久，这我们都知道；除非很快得手，这一局便输定了。可我们照旧一步一步往前拱，像梦里的人，时辰全然失了数，黑夜白昼混作一团。说真的，这一仗把我们的感官整个占去了；别处一派用场，它们便开始失灵。耳语也听得见的当口，我们却在彼此喊叫；那只还抡得动锤的手，已不敢托付它把杯子送上嘴唇；偶尔瞥一眼腕表，那表倒像远在天边。

===Original===

At a quarter past nine the next morning, our new shaft was eight yards deep. And this alone shows that we were beside ourselves, for, though we had cut down the width, relying upon the crowbar to make this good, such progress was superhuman. Yet, to advance this shaft further seemed little worth, and we started another tunnel out of its right-hand wall. This by Hanbury’s direction; for Mansel was resting, and we others knew no more where to turn than the man in the moon.

“Are you certain,” said I, “that we’re not too far to the left?”

“Certain,” said Hanbury. “Mansel will bear me out. The shaft from the well to the chamber is not so steep as we thought, so we’ve aimed too much to the right. You mark my words: if we don’t strike the chamber this time, we shall hit the shaft.”

Such confidence did us good: but, when I roused Mansel, I saw him tighten his lips, and I knew that he had been hoping to be sent for before his time.

===Chinese===

第二天早晨九点一刻，新坑道已进深八码。单凭这一层，便见得我们已经神志失常；因为宽度虽已收窄，指望事后拿撬棍补足，这样的进度也不是人力。可是，把这条坑道再往前掘，已没多大意思；我们从它右边的墙上又起了一条岔道。这是奉了汉伯里的示下；因为曼塞尔在歇班，我们这些人往哪儿去，同月亮里的人一样摸不着头脑。

「你敢肯定，」我说，「我们不是偏左了？」

「敢肯定，」汉伯里说，「曼塞尔会替我作证。从井到石室的那条竖井，没有我们想的那么陡；所以我们瞄得偏右了。你记住我的话：这回要是还凿不着石室，就准撞上竖井。」

这样的信心叫我们受用：可我去叫醒曼塞尔的时候，见他抿紧了嘴唇；我知道，他原指望不到钟点便有人来叫他。

===Original===

I now know that I must have slept for nearly my hour, when I dreamed I was listening to Mansel broadcasting news and that a storm somewhere was blotting out what he said. I must have dreamed for some moments, for I was heavy with sleep, but at last I awoke, to find Tester barking like fury three feet away.

In an instant I had caught him up and had blundered through the postern to fall headlong over the breastwork with the dog in my arms. However I was up in a moment and made haste to close the gate: but I dared not fasten it closely, for fear of a bomb.

I had much ado to quiet Tester, who was seething with wrath; but, when I had done so and could listen, I heard no sound.

So we lay still for five minutes: then I heard Mansel’s voice.

“Are you at your post, Chandos?”

“I am,” said I.

===Chinese===

如今我知道，那一个钟头我差不多睡足了：只是梦里听见曼塞尔在电台上播新闻，不知哪里起了风暴，把他说话的声音盖没了。梦必是做了一阵，因为我睡得死沉；末了总算醒来，只见三英尺外泰斯特正狂吠不止。

一转眼我抱起他，跌跌撞撞出了边门，怀里搂着狗，一头绊倒在胸墙上。好在我立刻爬起，赶紧去关门：可我不敢闩死，怕的是炸弹。

我好容易才把满腔怒火的泰斯特安抚下去；等他静了、我能侧耳了，什么声音也没有。

于是我们静静伏了五分钟；随后我听见曼塞尔的声音。

「你在岗位上吗，钱多斯？」

「在，」我说。

===Original===

“What happened?”

“I’ve no idea,” said I. “I woke up to find Tester barking, and that’s as much as I know.”

“Stay where you are,” said he.

Then he unmasked the searchlight and turned the beam on to the trap. Presently he raked the dungeon, letting the beam discover the timber and piles of earth.

===Chinese===

「出了什么事？」

「不知道，」我说，「我一睁眼，泰斯特在叫；我知道的就这么些。」

「守着别动，」他说。

随后他揭开探照灯，把光柱对准活板门。片刻之后，他把地牢扫了一遍，让光柱照出木料和一堆堆的土。

===Original===

At length﻿—

“Let Tester go,” he said. “And you come in.”

The dog ran to him at once and jumped up to lick his face. Then he turned away and began to growl and bristle, *with his eyes on the trap*.

“Not much doubt about that,” said Mansel, stooping to make much of the dog. “Our friends have found the front door. I suppose they’re not quite ready, and that that’s why they shut it again. Well, we’re quite ready when they are, and, till then, we may as well work.”

Then he sent me to bathe and eat, and, when I came back, Hanbury was asleep in the dungeon, with Tester in the crook of his arm.

We gave no more time to the riddle, for in truth we had none to give. The business smacked of a nightmare: yet, our present life was a dream: and so, if we thought, we did not speak of it, but tacitly took it for granted that Mansel’s interpretation was good.

===Chinese===

末了——

「放了泰斯特，」他说，「你进来。」

狗立刻跑到他跟前，跳起来舔他的脸。随后他转过身去，开始低吼、炸毛，*眼睛盯着活板门*。

「这没多大疑问，」曼塞尔说着，弯下腰去抚慰那条狗。「朋友们找着了前门。我猜他们还没预备停当，所以才又关上了。好，他们几时来，我们几时都齐备；在那之前，咱们只管干活。」

随后他打发我去洗澡吃饭；我回来时，汉伯里正睡在地牢里，泰斯特卧在他的臂弯里。

这桩哑谜，我们不再费神去猜，因为实在没有闲工夫可给。那件事带着一股噩梦的气味；可我们眼下的日子，本就是一场梦：所以即便心里想过，也不说出口，只是默认曼塞尔的解释不错。

===Original===

At six o’clock that evening we started another shaft.

The last we had driven five yards, and had sunk a three-feet crowbar into its nose. *And found nothing.* And so at six o’clock we started another shaft. This was driven from the nose of our second, so that our original tunnel would soon be a left-handed fork with three five-yard prongs.

And here I am bound to record that we were beginning to fail. I will swear that the spirit was willing: but the flesh was beginning to flag. It was nearly forty-five hours since Mansel and I had returned from our reconnaissance, and, though in that time we had each had ten hours’ sleep, the reconnaissance had come at the end of a full day’s toil. We had, therefore, been jaded when we began to spurt: and our spurt was losing its sting, because it did not end.

We were beginning to fail.

I knew that my strength was failing, and tried to conceal the fact. I fancy the others did the same, for the collapse of one must mean the end of our effort. The camel’s back would have broken: not even Mansel could have carried another straw.

===Chinese===

那天傍晚六点，我们又开了一条坑道。

上一条我们掘了五码，把一根三英尺的撬棍扎进它的坑道头。*什么也没碰着。*于是六点整，我们另起一条坑道。这一条从第二条的坑道头接出去；这样，我们最初那条坑道不久便要变成一柄朝左的叉子，生着三根五码长的齿。

写到这里，我不能不记下：我们开始垮了。我敢起誓，心是肯的；可肉体开始败下阵来。从曼塞尔和我侦察回来，已近四十五个钟头；虽然这期间我们每人睡了十个钟头，可侦察是在干足一整天活之后去的。我们起跑冲刺的时候，本是强弩之末；冲刺又迟迟不到头，劲头便一天天钝下去。

我们开始垮了。

我知道自己的力气在漏，竭力遮掩。我想旁人也在遮掩；因为一个人倒下，我们的事业便到了头。骆驼的背早已压断：再添一根稻草，连曼塞尔也驮不动。

===Original===

When I roused him at half past seven, he held up two canvas kit-bags for me to see.

“The Burglar’s Delight,” said he, with half a laugh.

I tried to laugh back, and lay down﻿—but not to sleep. And there was the surest sign that the end was at hand, for it showed that the flesh was rebelling against its chastisement.

When Mansel returned from the gallery, he stopped to peer at me. I pretended slumber: but his action showed me that he, too, had not slept.

I think the hour that followed was the worst I have ever spent. I was so sick and weary that I would have sold a kingdom for unconsciousness: but this was steadfastly withheld: and, though at times I fell into a kind of doze, this state was more dreadful than my first, for then my brain was unruly and flitted and gambolled, as a gnat on a summer’s eve.

===Chinese===

七点半我去叫他；他举起两只帆布装备袋给我看。

「盗贼的至爱（The Burglar's Delight），」他说，笑了一半。

我想回他一个笑，躺下了——却不是为了睡。这便是顶可靠的兆头：大限已近；因为它说明，肉体已经起来反抗这番苦役了。

曼塞尔从画廊回来，停住脚步端详我。我装睡；可他这一站，告诉我他也没睡。

接下来的那个钟头，我想是我平生熬过的最坏的一个钟头。我浑身病着、乏着，肯拿一个王国去换无知无觉；可这无知无觉偏不来：偶尔坠入半睡，那情形比先前更可怕，因为那时我的脑子不听管束，飘来荡去、蹦蹦跳跳，像夏日傍晚的一只蚊蚋。

===Original===

I was, indeed, thankful when Hanbury limped into the dungeon and I could go back in his place.

The gallery we were now driving was completing the uppermost prong of the letter E, the upright of which was formed by our second shaft and the base, or bottom prong, by the last five yards of our first. But, because of all our soundings, the works had a shapeless look and seemed to reflect the frenzy with which they had been done. The roof and walls were eccentric, and the timbers were all awry: indeed the carpenter’s task was now fit work for a wizard, and faithfully to prop and retain such irregular excavation was almost impossible.

At half past ten that night our new shaft was two yards deep. Mansel, Hanbury and I were working alone, for Bell had just left to rouse Rowley and take his place. And all was quiet; for Mansel had stopped for a moment to drink his wine, Hanbury was pencilling a timber, which he was going to saw, and I, who was hewing, was extracting a morsel of dirt, which had made its way into my eye.

===Chinese===

汉伯里一瘸一拐走进地牢、我能回去顶他的班时，我真是感激不尽。

我们那条新坑道，是在补全字母 E 最上头的一横：那一竖是第二条坑道，最底下那一横，是最初那条坑道的末五码。可是，因为探了这许多路，工程已是一副不成形状的模样，倒像把干它时的那股狂乱照出了模样。顶板和两壁歪歪扭扭，木料全都支得别扭：木匠的活计如今只配交给巫师；要把这样不规矩的洞子稳稳撑住、挡住，几乎办不到。

那晚十点半，新坑道进了两码。曼塞尔、汉伯里和我三个人在干活，因为贝尔刚去叫罗利顶班。四下里静悄悄：曼塞尔停下来呷一口酒，汉伯里在往一根木料上画铅笔线，预备下锯；抡镐的我，正在剔出一粒钻进眼睛的土屑。

===Original===

At first I thought Hanbury’s pencil was making a scraping noise: then I saw he had stopped and was listening and that Mansel was doing the same.

For a moment no one of us moved.

Then Hanbury stepped to my side and set his ear to a hole which the crowbar had made a foot back in the left-hand wall.

“That’s right,” said he, after a moment. “It’s coming from here.”

Then he stood away, and I made play with the pickaxe about the hole.

I had soon made a hollow, in which by sinking the crowbar we should gain another foot: but, before we did this, Mansel tore off his zephyr and folded it into a pad which should muffle the sound of the blows.

===Chinese===

起先我当那刮擦声是汉伯里的铅笔：随后我见他停了手，侧耳在听，曼塞尔也听了起来。

片刻之间，我们谁也没动。

随后汉伯里跨到我身边，把耳朵贴上左壁的一个孔——那是撬棍在退后一英尺的地方杵出来的。

过了一会儿，他说：「对了，声音打这儿来。」

随后他闪开身子，我围着那孔抡起镐来。

不多时我便凿出一个窝；把撬棍往里一送，还能再进一英尺：可下手之前，曼塞尔扯下他的薄纱衫（zephyr），叠成一块垫子，好把锤击的声音裹住。

===Original===

Gently I drove the bar home, and could almost have pressed it for the last foot of its way: and I drew it out with my hands without any effort at all.

The noise was distinct now﻿—a thin, regular murmur, as if someone was whetting a chisel upon a hone.

What it was I could not imagine, and was just beginning to think that our calculations had led us to the top of the well, when Mansel let out a sob and caught us each by an arm.

“*My God, I’ve got it,*” he cried. “*That’s the chamber ahead.* **And they’re Filing the Bars.**”

===Chinese===

我轻轻把撬棍送到底；末了那一英尺，简直拿手按都按得进去：抽出来时，双手毫不费力。

那声音如今清楚可辨——又细又匀的低响，仿佛有人在磨石上磨一把凿子。

这到底是什么，我想不出来；正开始疑心我们的测算竟把我们带到了井的上头，曼塞尔忽然哽咽一声，一手抓住了我们每人一条胳膊。

「*我的天，我听出来了，*」他喊道，「*前头就是石室。* **他们正在锉铁条。**」

---

===Original===

There is, I believe, a height at which a man’s heart will break: and so, I suppose, there is a pitch of excitement at which a man’s brain will balk. And I think we had come to this: for Mansel was trembling as a man smitten with an ague; if I had tried to speak, I should have broken down; and, while we were standing thus silent, Hanbury’s knees sagged and he fell down in a swoon.

The faint was nothing, and, before I had brought the bucket which was standing ten paces away, George was again on his feet: but we made him dip his head in the water, and then Mansel and I did the same.

Then we fell to, like madmen, to deepen the breach I had made.

===Chinese===

我想，人的心，苦到某个高度会碎；那么，想必也有一个兴奋的顶点，到了那里，人的脑子便不肯再走。我想我们已经到了这一步：曼塞尔抖得像害疟疾的病人；我若想说话，准会垮下来；我们这样默然立着的时候，汉伯里膝盖一软，昏倒在地。

昏厥算不得什么；我还没把十步开外立着的那只水桶拎来，乔治已经重新站直了：可我们还是按着他的头浸进水里；随后曼塞尔和我如法炮制。

随后我们发疯似的干起来，把我开出的豁口往深里凿。

===Original===

We worked in what silence we could and let the carpentry go: each of us hewed for two minutes, while the others withdrew his winnings and strewed them about the shafts: now and again we had to employ the shovel, but mostly we used our hands, so as to make less noise.

All the time the noise of the filing went steadily on, only ceasing from time to time to instantly recommence. Each time that it stopped our hearts went into our mouths: for, close as we were, if once the bars were severed, we might have been five miles off for all the good we could do.

When Rowley came back, we told him and sent him back to tell Bell.

“And say,” said Mansel, “that I may not arrive when I should, but that, whatever happens, he must remain where he is: for now the case is altered, and he and Tester are holding our line of retreat.”

===Chinese===

我们能多静便多静地干，木工活全撂下了：每人凿两分钟，其余的人把凿下的土清走，撒在各条坑道里；时不时还得动用铁锹，可多半用双手，好少出些声响。

锉声一直沉稳地响着，只是偶尔停一停，随即又立刻响起。每停一回，我们的心便提到嗓子眼：因为我们虽然近在咫尺，铁条只要一断，我们纵有通天的本事，也同远在五英里外没有两样。

罗利回来，我们把消息告诉了他，又打发他回去告诉贝尔。

「还要说，」曼塞尔说，「我未必能按钟点赶到；可无论出什么事，他都得守在原地：如今情形变了，他同泰斯特守着的是我们的退路。」

===Original===

Rowley was back in two minutes and wild to take his turn: and, once he had got it, he would not surrender the pickaxe, and I had fairly to wrest it out of his hands.

Now what the time was when it happened I do not know﻿—for I cannot tell to an hour how long we took to cut through that last three feet﻿—but I know that I launched the pickaxe and its head went out of my sight and that there I was, looking through a hole into an empty space, beyond which, when they gave me the light, I could see a stone wall.

It was the chamber indeed.

At once I saw that the well-diggers’ excavation had been bigger than the chamber itself and that they had not lined the cavity which they had dug, but had built the chamber within it, like a box within a box.

===Chinese===

罗利两分钟便回来了，一心要抢他的班：既得了手，镐便不肯撒手；我只好硬从他手里夺过来。

事情发生时是几点，我不知道——最后那三英尺凿了多久，我说不出一个钟点的上下——我只记得：我一镐打出去，镐头从我的视线里消失，眼前出现一个孔，孔外是一片空阔；他们把灯递给我，我便望见空阔那边立着一堵石墙。

正是石室，一点不假。

我立刻看出：掘井人挖的空穴比石室本身还大；他们没有给挖出的空穴砌衬，而是在里面另砌起一座石室，如同箱中套箱。

===Original===

There was now no mistaking the whine of iron biting iron, and it sounded to our frantic ears as though whosoever was filing was nearing the end of his task.

We, therefore, fell to, like fury, and soon had a ragged window, I suppose, some three feet square, opening into the cavern in which the chamber stood.

A moment’s inspection now showed that the chamber was round, like the well, and was plainly constructed of stones which had been cut by the masons to build the walls of the well. To break out of the chamber, therefore, would have been ten times as simple as to break in; for the stones were undoubtedly wedge-shaped, and, that being so, if they were truly laid, a battering-ram itself would not avail us. The joints moreover, were as fine as those of the walls of the well, and to cut out one stone with a chisel would have taken an hour or more.

“What of the roof?” whispered Mansel.

===Chinese===

铁啃铁的锐啸再也错认不得；在我们狂乱的耳朵听来，仿佛执锉的人已近完工。

于是我们发狂似的干起来；不多时便开出一个参差的窗洞——我想约莫三英尺见方——通到石室立着的那个洞窟里。

细看一眼，便看出石室是圆的，同井一样；砌它的石头，分明就是石匠为砌井壁凿的那些料。因此，若从石室里往外破，要比从外头破进去容易十倍；那些石头无疑是楔形的；果真如此，砌得又严实，便是攻城槌也帮不了我们的忙。再说，石缝又细得同井壁的一样；拿凿子剔一块石头，得花一个钟头还多。

「顶上怎么样？」曼塞尔悄声问。

===Original===

At once I stretched up an arm, to find the roof just out of sight; but two minutes’ work with the pickaxe had laid the edge of it bare.

Now how the roof was constructed I do not know; but between the slab we could see and the stones upon which it was resting, there was a layer of mortar as thick as a Camembert cheese. And this was so loose that I picked out a piece with my thumb.

Here, then, was the way to break in: for we had but to drive a chisel, and, when it was fairly in, to lever against the slab, to prize a stone out of the wall: and, once one stone was out, we could make our breach.

“And, when we do,” breathed Mansel, “look out for squalls: you can bet your life they’re not going to give us this trick.”

===Chinese===

我立刻扬起一条胳膊，摸到顶恰在视线之外；两分钟的镐工，便把它的边缘剥露了出来。

至于顶是怎么筑的，我不知道；可我们望得见的那块石板，同托着它的石头之间，夹着一层灰浆，厚得像一块卡芒贝尔奶酪；而且松得很，我用拇指便抠下了一块。

这样，破门的路数便有了：只消把凿子打进去，楔稳当了，抵着石板一撬，便能把一块石头从墙上起出来：抽出一块，豁口就好开了。

「真到那一天，」曼塞尔低低地说，「就当心风雨：拿性命打赌，他们绝不会白白送我们这个便宜。」

===Original===

We had to make way for the lever: so once more I handled the pickaxe, whilst the others marshalled the tools for the final assault.

All this time the filing continued, and, to judge from the ring of the metal, some bar was nearly in two. Indeed, as I threw down the pickaxe, again the noise stopped, and we heard some blows administered, as though the workman believed he could burst asunder the filament that remained.

We waited to hear no more.

I fitted the edge of the chisel into the chink; and, whilst I held it, Mansel hammered it home.
